max ∑ g_i

Let's consider discrete algorithm with many parameters. We can maybe use "low peak time-domain phasing" by optimizing a sequence of nonnegative values with smooth objective log-sum-exp. We can also use messages: Since F=max c, we can solve via "winner-take-all" iterative scaling: when c_k too high, reduce a_i a_{k-i} products by scaling down factors. Multiplicative update: For current a, compute c. For each active k, gradient. Multiplicative weights for nonnegative quadratic objectives often: a_i ← a_i * exp(-η ∑_k w_k ∂c_k/∂a_i) then normalize. This is like minimizing ∑ w_k c_k. If choose w_k based on c_k (e.g., w_k=1 if c_k>threshold), it may flatten.

Let's design an effective heuristic:
- Normalize a sum=1.
- Compute c = conv(a,a).
- Define active weights w = (c / max_c)^β, β large (e.g., 20) normalized sum=1. Then J=∑ w_k c_k (weighted sum, emphasizes peaks). Gradient g = ∑ w_k ∂c_k = computed via correlation and prefix sums.
- Update log a: a ← a * exp(-η g) / sum. (Since J quadratic, use gradient descent in log space with step). Choose η adaptively.
This is like exponential weighting; should reduce peaks. But if g has large variations, may zero out some coordinates. We can clip lower bound (e.g., 1e-12) to keep support.

Need compute gradient efficiently.
J = ∑_k w_k ∑_i a_i a_{k-i}. Derivative as above: dJ/da_j = ∑_k w_k [a_{k-j} + a_j] for k where 0≤k-j<n. Let term1_j = ∑_i a_i w_{i+j}. term2_j = a_j S_j where S_j=∑_{k=max(0,j)}^{min(2n-2,j+n-1)} w_k.
Then grad = term1 + term2. Note term1 = np.correlate(w, a, 'valid')? Need verify orientation.
Let's test n=3, a=[a0,a1,a2], w=[w0,w1,w2,w3,w4]. term1_j by formula: j=0: i=0 a0 w0 + i=1 a1 w1 + i=2 a2 w2. correlate(w,a,'valid')? np.correlate(w,a,valid) returns length 3: for offset? Our term1_j=1 would be a0 w1 + a1 w2 + a2 w3. Same. Good. So term1 = np.correlate(w, a, mode='valid') (w first). But careful with dtype.
term2 S_j: for j=0: k=0..2? condition k∈[0,n-1]=[0,2], yes sum w0+w1+w2. For j=1: k=1..3 sum w1..3. j=2: k=2..4 sum w2..4. Prefix sums of w length 2n-1.

def conv_full(a):
    n=len(a); L=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()? Need length at least 2n-1. For real, use rfft. But next power of two maybe memory small: n=4806 ->2n-2=9610 bit_length 14 => 16384. rfft length L, operations okay.
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    return np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=L)[:2*n-1]

Term1 via FFT:
def cross_valid(w, a): # returns term1 length n
    n=len(a)
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    # want y[k] = sum_i a_i * w_rev[k-i] with convolution
    # L >= len(a)+len(w_rev)-1 = n+(2n-1)-1=3n-2
    L=1 << (3*n-3).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, L); fw=np.fft.rfft(w_rev, L)
    y=irfft(fa*fw,L)
    # term1_j = y[2n-2-j]
    return y[2*n-2 :: -1][:n]
Check with small n=3: a=[a0,a1,a2], w length5. w_rev indices 0..4: w4,w3,w2,w1,w0.

But y may have length > 6. okay.

Let's derive: Want minimize max_k (g*g)_k. Linearize at a:

Implement fast_convolve:
def convolve_full_fft(a):
    n=a.shape[0]
    L=1
    need=2*n-1
    while L<need: L <<=1
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa, n=L)
    return c[:need]
Potential numerical: c small negative; for max use np.max(c) maybe if negative? c[0]=a0² nonnegative. For gradient weights, c should be nonnegative; clip c = np.maximum(c, 0). But if FFT negative small, okay.

Compute gradient:
def gradient_w_logsum(a,w):
    n=len(a)
    # term1 via FFT correlation
    # Could also use direct if n small? We'll implement FFT.
    Lw = 1; need=3*n-2; while Lw<need: Lw<<=1
    a_rev? Need w_rev.
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a, n=Lw)
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w_rev, n=Lw)
    y=np.fft.irfft(fa*fw, n=Lw)
    term1 = y[2*n-2 :: -1][:n]  # y[4?]
    # But due FFT, y indices float; convert.
    # prefix sums of w length 2n-1
    cw = np.concatenate((np.zeros(1), np.cumsum(w)))
    # S_j = sum w[j:j+n] for j=0..n-1
    S = cw[j+n] - cw[j]? Need vectorized:
    j=np.arange(n)
    S = cw[j+n] - cw[j]
    return term1 + a*S
Check length: w length 2n-1. For j=n-1, j+n=2n-1 valid index in cw length 2n. Good.
Term1 formula: y[2n-2-j]. For j=0 index2n-2; j=n-1 index n-1. y should have nonzeros sufficiently. We take slice descending. In numpy y[2*n-2 :: -1][:n] works if index exists up to 2n-2. y length Lw. Good.

Softmax weights:
def softmax_weights(c, beta):
    # c nonnegative; want w=exp(beta*c / maxc?)
    m = max(c)
    if m<=0: return uniform
    x = beta * c / m
    x -= x.max() # x.max=beta
    w = np.exp(x)
    w /= w.sum()
    return w
Beta maybe 10. For flat c, w near uniform; for peaked, concentrated.

Update:
def multiplicative_update(a,w,r, floor):
    grad=gradient...
    gscale = grad.mean()
    if gscale<=0: return a
    logfac = np.exp(-r * grad / gscale)
    a2 = a * logfac
    a2 = np.maximum(a2, floor)
    a2 /= a2.sum()
    return a2
But grad/ gscale mean 1, r relative step. r maybe 0.2 to2. For nonconvex, line search.

def optimize_trial(a, time_limit, beta, noun?):
    a normalize.
    best=maxc; besta=a.copy()
    beta_current=beta
    r=0.7
    no_improve=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    it=0
    while time < limit - 2:
       c=convolve(a); maxc=c.max()
       if maxc < best - 1e-12: best=maxc; besta=a.copy(); no_improve=0; # maybe print
       else no_improve += maybe
       if no_improve > 100? adjust beta/r: if beta<50 beta*=1.5? If stagnation, perturb.
       w=softmax_weights(c,beta_current)
       # try steps [0.1*r, r, 3*r]? select best candidate by fast_score limited.
       candidates = []
       for f in [0.25,1.0,3.0]:
          a2=update(a,w,r*f)
          # maybe backtracking evaluate max only
       Choose best by fast_score among 1-2; to save, evaluate two: if f=1 score < current use; else try smaller/larger.
       Simpler: take a2=update with r; c2=conv; if max2<maxc accept with r unchanged/increase; else decay r.
       To escape, if no improvement for 30, add random perturbation: a = a*(1+0.02*normal) normalized; reset r.
       Also periodically mix with uniform: a = (1-0.05)*a +0.05/n.
    At end normalize and return besta.

Optimizer details:
We need a fast way to select candidate. Each iteration:
1. c = convolve(a); maxc.
2. Update best.
3. If maxc < target? continue.
4. w=softmax(c,beta). To ensure weights not too concentrated causing numerical issues, beta maybe 8-20.
5. grad = grad_logsum(a,w)
6. Normalize grad by mean. Let r = current step (e.g., 0.2). Candidate a1 = a*exp(-r*grad/gmean); floor; normalize.
7. Evaluate c1. If max1 < maxc - tol: accept, r = min(max_r, r*1.1); no_improve=0.
   Else evaluate a2 with r/2 maybe. If accept smaller, keep r or reduce. Else reduce r*=0.7; no_improve++.
   If no_improve > N: perturb, reset r.
Maybe also try a "max active" update: weights uniform on top 5% of c. This can be more aggressive. We can alternate: every 100 iterations use top_k weights to target peaks.

def smooth_perturb(a, rng, eps):
   n=len(a)
   noise=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1, 9):
       f = rng.uniform(1, max(2, 2*m))
       phase=rng.uniform(0,2pi)
       amp=eps/(m+0.5)
       noise += amp*np.sin(2π*f*a? + phase)
   factor=1+rng.normal(0,eps/3) + 0.1*noise
   f=np.maximum(factor,0.1)
   a2=a*f; a2 += 1e-8/n; normalize

make_fourier(n, rng, strength=0.7):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   h=np.zeros(n)
   comps = 6
   for m in range(1, comps+1):
      # frequencies integer to maintain anti? Use sin(2π m x), which is anti and zero endpoints.
      amp = strength/(m+0.5)
      sign = rng.choice([-1,1])
      h += sign*amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x)
   # scale h to max abs <=0.8
   if max|h|>0: h*=0.8/max
   a=1+h; a=max(a,1e-6); normalize

def smooth_perturb(a, rng, eps):
   n=len(a)
   x=np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1)
   noise=np.zeros(n)
   for m in range(1,7):
      # random frequency maybe integer? Use real to random.
      f = rng.uniform(1, 3*m+1)
      phase = rng.uniform(0, 2*np.pi)
      noise += (1/(m+0.5))*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x + phase)
   # optional random high freq small
   noise += 0.2*rng.normal(0,1,n)
   factor = 1.0 + eps*noise
   factor = np.maximum(factor, 0.2)
   a2 = a*factor
   a2 += 1e-12
   s=a2.sum(); if s>0 a2/=s
   return a2

Top weights:
def top_weights(c, frac=0.02):
   k=max(8, int(np.ceil(c.size*frac)))
   idx=np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]  # top k
   w=np.zeros(c.size,dtype=float); w[idx]=1.0/k; return w
Note if c has nan, fallback.

Softmax weights:
def softmax_weights(c,beta):
   m=c.max()
   if not finite or m<=0: uniform
   x=(beta*(c/m))
   x-=x.max()
   w=np.exp(x)
   s=w.sum(); if s<=0 uniform; w/=s
   return w

```
def enforce_anti(a):
    n=len(a); base=1.0/n
    for _ in range(2):
        d=0.5*(a - a[::-1])
        a=base + d
        a=np.maximum(a,0)
        s=a.sum()
        if s>0: a/=s
    return a
```

Let's define `apply_update`:
```
def apply_update(a, grad, gmean, step, floor, anti):
    z = step * grad / gmean
    # clip exponent to avoid under/overflow
    z = np.clip(z, -50, 50)
    a2 = a * np.exp(-z)
    if anti: a2 = enforce_anti(a2)
    else: a2 = np.maximum(a2, floor)
    s = a2.sum()
    if s <=0: return a.copy()
    a2 /= s
    a2 = np.maximum(a2, 0)
    return a2
```
If anti, enforce includes floor. Also after anti, maybe add floor? Since values around 1/n, floor not needed. For non-anti, floor.

Let's design `make_init`:
```
def make_init(n, tag, rng, anti=False):
   if n <=0: return None
   x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n,dtype=np.float64)
   if tag == 'linear':
       a = 1.0 + 0.5*(2*x-1)
       # small anti sine
       a = a + 0.1*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
   elif tag == 'anti_linear':
       amp = 0.5
       a = 1.0 + amp*(2*x-1)
   elif tag == 'anti_fourier' or tag == 'fourier':
       h=np.zeros(n)
       comps=8
       for m in range(1, comps+1):
          amp=0.7/(m+0.5)
          sign=1.0 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1.0
          h += sign*amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x)
       hm=np.max(np.abs(h))
       if hm > 1e-15: h *= 0.8/hm
       a = 1.0 + h
   elif tag == 'block':
       h = 0.5*(0.5*(1.0+np.tanh((0.7 - x)/0.08)) - 0.5*(1.0+np.tanh((x - 0.3)/0.08)))? simpler left high right low smooth:
       h = 0.5*(1.0/(1.0+np.exp((x-0.5)/0.05))) # logistic from1 to0
       h = h - 0.5
   else: a=np.ones(n)
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if anti: a=enforce_anti(a)
   else: a=np.maximum(a,0)
   return normalize_sequence(a)
```
Check block logistic: h= sigmoid*((1)/(1+exp((x-0.5)/0.05))) ranges ~1 left 0 right, subtract0.5 => ±0.5. Good.
For 'fourier' non-anti, we still use anti sine; if anti=False okay.

`enforce_anti` normalizes to sum1? It uses base 1/n. If input sum not1, project might not normalize pair mean to 1/n; but we call after normalize or before final normalize. In make_init anti, call enforce_anti then normalize. Implementation:
```
def enforce_anti(a):
    n=a.size
    base=1.0/n
    for _ in range(3):
        d=0.5*(a - a[::-1])
        a=base + d
        a=np.maximum(a,0.0)
        s=a.sum()
        if s>0 and np.isfinite(s): a/=s
    return a
```
This enforces sum1 but if clipping breaks anti, after second pass base+diff of normalized non-anti; final sum maybe not exactly 1 after last clip? We normalize inside each pass; after final normalize sum1. Good.

Potential issue: If input has values far outside [0,2/n], diff large, base+diff may be negative and clipped, effectively limiting amplitude. Good.

Now, `optimize_sequence`:
Pseudo with detailed code:
```
def optimize_sequence(a_start, deadline, rng, anti=False, beta=10.0, r0=0.35, tag=''):
    a=normalize_sequence(a_start)
    if a is None: return None, inf
    n=a.size
    if anti: a=enforce_anti(a)
    floor=max(1e-15, 1e-8/n)
    c=fft_convolve(a); c=np.maximum(c,0); maxc=c.max(); s0=a.sum(); score=2*n*maxc/(s0*s0)
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score
    current_r=r0
    no_improve=0
    iteration=0
    last_print=time.monotonic()
    print(f"[{tag}] n={n} start score={score:.6f}", flush=True)
    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
       c=fft_convolve(a)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)): break
       c=np.maximum(c,0)
       maxc=float(c.max()); s=float(a.sum()); score=2*n*maxc/(s*s)
       if not np.isfinite(score): break
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=score; best=a.copy()
           if best_score < 1.55 or iteration%500==0: print...
           no_improve=0
       else:
           no_improve += 1
           if no_improve == 25:
               if beta < 40: beta = beta*1.25 +1
           if no_improve == 70:
               # perturb
               a = smooth_perturb(a, rng, 0.1 if anti else 0.08)
               if anti: a=enforce_anti(a)
               current_r=0.3
               beta=10.0
               no_improve=0
           if no_improve == 120:
               # blend random init
               n=a.size
               rand=make_init(n, 'anti_fourier' if anti else 'fourier', rng, anti)
               if rand is not None:
                  for mix in (0.1,0.3,0.5):
                     a_try=(1-mix)*a + mix*rand
                     if anti: a_try=enforce_anti(a_try)
                     else: a_try=normalize_sequence(np.maximum(a_try,0))
                     if a_try is None: continue
                     s_try=fast_score(a_try)
                     if np.isfinite(s_try) and s_try < score:
                         a=a_try; break
               current_r=0.3; beta=10.0; no_improve=0
       # choose weights
       use_top = no_improve > 40 and (iteration % 2 == 0)
       if use_top:
           w=top_weights(c, 0.02)
       else:
           w=softmax_weights(c, beta)
       g=grad_weighted(a,w)
       if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)): g=np.ones(n)/n
       gmean=float(np.mean(g))
       if gmean <= 1e-300:
           # uniform gradient, cannot improve; perturb
           a=smooth_perturb(a,rng,0.1); if anti: a=enforce_anti(a); continue
       base_grad = g/gmean
       accepted=False
       # try step 1
       a1=apply_update(a, base_grad, current_r, floor, anti)
       s1=fast_score(a1)
       if np.isfinite(s1) and s1 < score - 1e-12:
           a=a1; score=s1; accepted=True
           if current_r < 1.2:
               a2=apply_update(a, base_grad, 2.0*current_r, floor, anti)
               s2=fast_score(a2)
               if np.isfinite(s2) and s2 < score - 1e-12:
                   a=a2; score=s2
           if best_score > score: ... but next loop updates
           current_r = min(2.0, current_r*1.15)
       else:
           # try half
           a_half=apply_update(a, base_grad, 0.5*current_r, floor, anti)
           sh=fast_score(a_half)
           if np.isfinite(sh) and sh < score - 1e-12:
               a=a_half; score=sh; accepted=True
               current_r = min(2.0, current_r*1.05)
           else:
               a_q=apply_update(a, base_grad, 0.25*current_r, floor, anti)
               sq=fast_score(a_q)
               if np.isfinite(sq) and sq < score - 1e-12:
                   a=a_q; score=sq; accepted=True
               else:
                   current_r *=0.65
       if time.monotonic() - last_print > 30:
           print(...); last_print=time.monotonic()
       iteration +=1
    # final enforce and score
    if anti: best=enforce_anti(best)
    best=normalize_sequence(best)
    if best is not None:
       best_score=fast_score(best)
       # if best_score inf? fallback
    print(end)
    return best, best_score
```
Note: In line search, after accepting a1 and trying 2r, we use base_grad computed from old a, not new. That's a secant-ish; may be okay but could be worse. Simpler: only use one candidate with chosen fac to avoid second-order issues. Let's reconsider.
If we use old grad and r, trying 2r from same old a acceptable. If accepted r, trying 2r from old a may overshoot but we evaluate. We set a to best of old a1 and old a2. Fine.
If we accept a_half, base_grad from old. Good.
But in code above after a1 accepted, we compute a2 from `a` (updated) using same base_grad, which is inconsistent. Use original `orig_g=base_grad`, `orig_a=a` before updates. For candidates:
```
orig_a=a
a1=apply_update(orig_a, base_grad, current_r)
best_cand=orig? score
if s1<score:
  a2=apply_update(orig_a, base_grad, min(2*current_r,3))
  ...
```
Better.

Implement:
```
facs=[]
if current_r < 1.0: facs=[1.0, 2.0]
else: facs=[1.0, 0.5]
# if facs none? add 1.0
```

```
def smooth_perturb(a, rng, eps, anti=False):
    n=a.size; x=np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1); noise=np.zeros(n)
    for m in range(1,8):
       if anti:
          f=int(rng.integers(1, 6*m+1))
          sign=1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1
          noise += sign*(1/(m+0.5))*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x)
       else:
          f=rng.uniform(1,6*m+1); phase=rng.uniform(0,2π)
          noise += (1/(m+0.5))*np.sin(2π f x + phase)
    # add small white noise? For anti, white noise should be anti: generate v and anti=(v-v[::-1])/2.
    if noise.size>0:
       noise += 0.2*(rng.normal(0,1,n) if not anti else (rng.normal(0,1,n)-rng.normal(0,1,n)[::-1])*0.5)
    factor=1+eps*noise
    factor=np.maximum(factor,0.2)
    a2=a*factor
    if anti: a2=enforce_anti(a2)
    else: a2=normalize_sequence(np.maximum(a2,0))
    if a2 is None: return a
    return a2
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time

"""
Search for a nonnegative sequence minimizing
    score = 2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / (sum(a)^2).

The program uses FFT-based exact convolution, smooth log-sum-exp / active-peak
subgradient multiplicative updates, projected anti-symmetric trials, and
multi-start search.  It starts from several structured initial guesses and
returns the best sequence found before the deadline.
"""


def next_power_of_two(need: int) -> int:
    if need <= 1:
        return 1
    p = 1
    while p < need:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def fft_convolve(a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0] * a[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    need = 2 * n - 1
    L = next_power_of_two(need)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, n=L)
    return c[:need].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def fft_correlation_valid(w: np.ndarray, a: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Returns out[j] = sum_i a[i] * w[i+j], for j = 0..n-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if n == 1:
        return np.array([float(a[0] * w[0])], dtype=np.float64)
    need = 3 * n - 2
    L = next_power_of_two(need)
    w_rev = w[::-1]
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, n=L)
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w_rev, n=L)
    y = np.fft.irfft(fa * fw, n=L)
    return y[2 * n - 2 :: -1][:n].astype(np.float64, copy=False)


def grad_weighted(a: np.ndarray, w: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Gradient of J(a) = sum_k w[k] * (convolve(a,a))[k].
    a length n, w length 2n-1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size != 2 * n - 1:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    term1 = fft_correlation_valid(w, a)

    cw = np.empty(w.size + 1, dtype=np.float64)
    cw[0] = 0.0
    np.cumsum(w, out=cw[1:])
    j = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)
    window_sums = cw[j + n] - cw[j]

    g = term1 + a * window_sums
    return np.maximum(g, 0.0)


def normalize_sequence(a, floor: float = 0.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    if floor > 0.0:
        a = np.maximum(a, floor)
    else:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return None
    a = a / s
    return a


def fast_score(a) -> float:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("inf")
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    c = fft_convolve(a)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m < 0.0:
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s))


def softmax_weights(c: np.ndarray, beta: float) -> np.ndarray:
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(m) or m <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(c.size, 1.0 / max(1, c.size), dtype=np.float64)
    x = beta * (c / m)
    x -= float(np.max(x))
    w = np.exp(x)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        return np.full(c.size, 1.0 / max(1, c.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return w / s


def top_weights(c: np.ndarray, frac: float = 0.02) -> np.ndarray:
    c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    k = int(max(8, min(c.size, max(1, round(c.size * float(frac))))))
    if c.size <= 1:
        return np.ones(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    if k >= c.size:
        return np.full(c.size, 1.0 / c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    idx = np.argpartition(c, -k)[-k:]
    w = np.zeros(c.size, dtype=np.float64)
    w[idx] = 1.0 / max(1, k)
    return w


def enforce_anti(a) -> np.ndarray:
    """
    Project onto a_i + a_{n-1-i} = 2/n, nonnegative, sum 1.
    """
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    n = a.size
    base = 1.0 / n
    for _ in range(3):
        d = 0.5 * (a - a[::-1])
        a = base + d
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            a = np.full(n, base, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            a = a / s
    return a


def apply_update(a: np.ndarray, rel_grad: np.ndarray, step: float,
                 floor: float, anti: bool) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    rel_grad = np.asarray(rel_grad, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    z = float(step) * rel_grad
    z = np.where(np.isfinite(z), z, 0.0)
    z = np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)
    a2 = a * np.exp(-z)

    if anti:
        a2 = enforce_anti(a2)
    else:
        a2 = np.maximum(a2, float(floor))
        s = float(np.sum(a2))
        if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
            return a.copy()
        a2 = a2 / s
    return a2


def make_init(n: int, tag: str, rng, anti: bool = False):
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    n = int(n)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)

    if tag == "linear":
        # A mild anti-symmetric ramp plus one full sine to break exact plateau behavior.
        h = 0.45 * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
        h += 0.12 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        a = 1.0 + h

    elif tag == "anti_linear":
        h = 0.55 * (2.0 * x - 1.0)
        h += 0.10 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        a = 1.0 + h

    elif tag == "anti_fourier" or tag == "fourier":
        h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        comps = 10
        for m in range(1, comps + 1):
            amp = 0.65 / (m + 0.5)
            sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
            # Full-period sine modes are anti-symmetric about the center.
            h += sign * amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x)
        hm = float(np.max(np.abs(h))) if h.size else 0.0
        if hm > 1e-15:
            h *= 0.85 / hm
        a = 1.0 + h

    elif tag == "block":
        # Smooth step left-high/right-low, then centered to zero mean.
        w = max(0.02, 0.08 * (1.0 / (1.0 + 0.1 * n)))
        sig = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp((x - 0.5) / w))
        h = sig - 0.5
        h += 0.08 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
        a = 1.0 + h

    else:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)

    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)

    if anti:
        a = enforce_anti(a)
    else:
        a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
        a = normalize_sequence(a, floor=1e-12)
        if a is None:
            a = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return a


def smooth_perturb(a: np.ndarray, rng, eps: float, anti: bool = False) -> np.ndarray:
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return a
    x = np.arange(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n - 1)
    noise = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

    for m in range(1, 8):
        if anti:
            f = int(rng.integers(1, 6 * m + 1))
            sign = 1.0 if rng.random() < 0.5 else -1.0
            noise += sign * (1.0 / (m + 0.5)) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(f) * x)
        else:
            f = float(rng.uniform(1.0, 6.0 * m + 1.0))
            phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            noise += (1.0 / (m + 0.5)) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * x + phase)

    if anti:
        v = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
        noise += 0.25 * 0.5 * (v - v[::-1])
    else:
        noise += 0.25 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)

    factor = 1.0 + float(eps) * noise
    factor = np.maximum(factor, 0.2)
    a2 = a * factor

    if anti:
        a2 = enforce_anti(a2)
    else:
        a2 = normalize_sequence(np.maximum(a2, 0.0), floor=1e-12)
        if a2 is None:
            return a
    return a2


def optimize_sequence(a_start, deadline: float, rng, anti: bool = False,
                      beta: float = 10.0, r0: float = 0.35, tag: str = ""):
    a = normalize_sequence(a_start, floor=1e-12)
    if a is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    if anti:
        a = enforce_anti(a)

    n = a.size
    if n <= 0:
        return None, float("inf")

    floor = max(1e-14, 1e-9 / n)

    c = fft_convolve(a)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    score = float(2.0 * n * float(np.max(c)) / (s * s)) if s > 0 else float("inf")

    best = a.copy()
    best_score = score if np.isfinite(score) else float("inf")

    current_r = float(r0)
    no_improve = 0
    iteration = 0
    last_print = time.monotonic()
    start_score = best_score

    print(f"[{tag}] n={n} anti={anti} start_best={best_score:.9f}", flush=True)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline - 2.0:
        c = fft_convolve(a)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(c)):
            break
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        m = float(np.max(c))
        s = float(np.sum(a))
        score = float(2.0 * n * m / (s * s)) if s > 0 else float("inf")
        if not np.isfinite(score):
            break

        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = score
            best = a.copy()
            no_improve = 0
        else:
            no_improve += 1
            if no_improve == 30:
                beta = min(60.0, beta * 1.25 + 1.0)
            if no_improve == 80:
                a = smooth_perturb(a, rng, 0.10, anti)
                if anti:
                    a = enforce_anti(a)
                else:
                    a = normalize_sequence(a, floor=1e-12)
                    if a is None:
                        break
                current_r = 0.3
                beta = 10.0
                no_improve = 0
                continue
            if no_improve == 160:
                rand = make_init(n, "anti_fourier" if anti else "fourier", rng, anti)
                if rand is not None:
                    accepted_blend = False
                    for mix in (0.08, 0.20, 0.35):
                        a_try = (1.0 - mix) * a + mix * rand
                        if anti:
                            a_try = enforce_anti(a_try)
                        else:
                            a_try = normalize_sequence(np.maximum(a_try, 0.0), floor=1e-12)
                        if a_try is None:
                            continue
                        s_try = fast_score(a_try)
                        if np.isfinite(s_try) and s_try < score - 1e-12:
                            a = a_try
                            accepted_blend = True
                            break
                    if accepted_blend:
                        score = fast_score(a)
                        if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                            best_score = score
                            best = a.copy()
                current_r = 0.3
                beta = 10.0
                no_improve = 0
                continue

        use_top = (no_improve > 45) and (iteration % 2 == 0)
        if use_top:
            w = top_weights(c, 0.018)
        else:
            w = softmax_weights(c, beta)

        g = grad_weighted(a, w)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            g = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        gmean = float(np.mean(g))
        if gmean <= 1e-300:
            a = smooth_perturb(a, rng, 0.12, anti)
            if anti:
                a = enforce_anti(a)
            else:
                a = normalize_sequence(a, floor=1e-12)
                if a is None:
                    break
            current_r = 0.3
            continue

        rel = g / gmean
        if float(np.std(rel)) < 1e-8:
            a = smooth_perturb(a, rng, 0.12, anti)
            if anti:
                a = enforce_anti(a)
            else:
                a = normalize_sequence(a, floor=1e-12)
                if a is None:
                    break
            current_r = 0.3
            continue

        orig_a = a
        orig_score = score
        facs = [1.0, 2.0, 0.5] if current_r < 1.0 else [1.0, 0.5]

        cand = None
        cand_score = orig_score
        for fac in facs:
            a_try = apply_update(orig_a, rel, current_r * fac, floor, anti)
            if a_try is None:
                continue
            s_try = fast_score(a_try)
            if np.isfinite(s_try) and s_try < cand_score - 1e-12:
                cand = a_try
                cand_score = s_try

        if cand is None:
            a_try = apply_update(orig_a, rel, 0.25 * current_r, floor, anti)
            s_try = fast_score(a_try)
            if np.isfinite(s_try) and s_try < cand_score - 1e-12:
                cand = a_try
                cand_score = s_try

        if cand is None:
            current_r *= 0.65
            if current_r < 0.02:
                a = smooth_perturb(a, rng, 0.18, anti)
                if anti:
                    a = enforce_anti(a)
                else:
                    a = normalize_sequence(a, floor=1e-12)
                    if a is None:
                        break
                current_r = 0.3
        else:
            a = cand
            score = cand_score
            if orig_score > 0.0 and score < orig_score * 0.999:
                current_r = min(2.5, current_r * 1.10)
            else:
                current_r = min(2.5, current_r * 1.01)
            if score < best_score - 1e-12:
                best_score = score
                best = a.copy()

        if time.monotonic() - last_print > max(20.0, deadline - time.monotonic()):
            print(
                f"[{tag}] n={n} it={iteration} score={score:.9f} "
                f"best={best_score:.9f} r={current_r:.3f} beta={beta:.2f} "
                f"left={max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()):.1f}s",
                flush=True
            )
            last_print = time.monotonic()

        iteration += 1

    if anti:
        best = enforce_anti(best)
    else:
        best = normalize_sequence(best, floor=1e-12)
        if best is None:
            best = np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)

    final_score = fast_score(best)
    if not np.isfinite(final_score) or final_score > best_score:
        final_score = best_score

    print(
        f"[{tag}] n={n} done it={iteration} final={final_score:.9f} "
        f"start={start_score:.9f}",
        flush=True
    )
    return best, final_score


def fallback_sequence(seed=42):
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    a = make_init(4096, "linear", rng, False)
    if a is None:
        a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(a))
    if s <= 0:
        a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0
    else:
        a /= s
    a = np.minimum(a, 1000.0)
    return [float(v) for v in a]


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = hard_deadline - 18.0
    if deadline - t0 < 30.0:
        return fallback_sequence(seed)

    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    best_a = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    height = None
    try:
        height_seq = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        if height_seq is not None:
            h = np.asarray(height_seq, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
            if h.size > 0:
                h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
                h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
                hs = float(np.sum(h))
                if hs > 0:
                    height = h / hs
    except Exception:
        height = None

    plans = []
    weights = []

    if height is not None and 128 <= height.size <= 20000:
        n_h = height.size
        lin_h = make_init(n_h, "linear", rng, False)
        if lin_h is not None:
            start_h = normalize_sequence(0.82 * height + 0.18 * lin_h, floor=1e-12)
        else:
            start_h = height.copy()
        plans.append((n_h, start_h, False, "height_mix"))
        weights.append(0.06)
    else:
        plans.append((4096, make_init(4096, "linear", rng, False), False, "lin4096"))
        weights.append(0.06)

    fixed = [
        (8192, "anti_linear", True, 0.24),
        (8192, "anti_fourier", True, 0.24),
        (16384, "linear", False, 0.16),
        (4096, "anti_linear", True, 0.10),
        (4096, "linear", False, 0.10),
        (8192, "block", False, 0.10),
    ]
    for n, tag, anti, w in fixed:
        if n < 128 or n > 32768:
            continue
        a0 = make_init(n, tag, rng, anti)
        if a0 is None:
            continue
        plans.append((n, a0, anti, tag))
        weights.append(w)

    total_w = float(sum(weights))
    if total_w <= 0:
        return fallback_sequence(seed)

    initial_remaining = max(30.0, deadline - t0 - 30.0)

    for idx, (n, a0, anti, tag) in enumerate(plans):
        now = time.monotonic()
        if deadline - now < 35.0:
            break
        trial_budget = initial_remaining * (weights[idx] / total_w)
        trial_deadline = min(deadline, now + max(2.0, trial_budget))
        if trial_deadline - now < 2.0:
            continue

        try:
            a_best, s_best = optimize_sequence(
                a0, trial_deadline, rng, anti=anti, beta=10.0, r0=0.35, tag=tag
            )
        except Exception as exc:
            print(f"[{tag}] exception: {exc}", flush=True)
            a_best, s_best = None, float("inf")

        if a_best is not None and np.isfinite(s_best):
            if s_best < best_score:
                best_score = s_best
                best_a = a_best

    if best_a is None:
        best_a = make_init(4096, "linear", rng, False)
        if best_a is None:
            best_a = np.ones(4096, dtype=np.float64) / 4096.0

    best_a = np.asarray(best_a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    best_a = np.where(np.isfinite(best_a), best_a, 0.0)
    best_a = np.maximum(best_a, 0.0)
    best_a = np.minimum(best_a, 1000.0)
    s = float(np.sum(best_a))
    if s <= 0:
        out = fallback_sequence(seed)
    else:
        best_a = best_a / s
        # Very small values are harmless, but avoid underflow when converted.
        best_a = np.where(best_a > 1e-300, best_a, 0.0)
        out = [float(v) for v in best_a]

    return out
```
